From student admissions to generation of financial aid letters, the volume of work and associated processing is linked to the academic calendar, creating uneven demand for resources.</p><p>Ellucian Banner maintains student, faculty, course, alumni, financial, financial aid and personnel data, and students can use Banner directly to register for classes, check grades, and access direct deposit information as related to disbursed aid.</p><p>“We have long used Applications Manager to automate Banner functions, which saves my team a great deal of time, especially during our busiest periods like fall admissions,” said Stacey Bocard, Systems Applications Manager. “However, even though we have significant experience in how Banner and Applications Manager work together, working with AutomWorx gave us access to an even higher level of expertise.”</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-037b070 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"037b070\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">Using Consulting</h4>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-680074d elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"680074d\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>AutomWorx offers assistance with implementations, upgrades, health checks, licensing, and extended support to organizations who utilize automation platforms like Applications Manager and Automic. Indiana State University, for instance, leveraged consulting to migrate their environment to virtual machines.</p><p>“AutomWorx was easy to work with, flexible, and very knowledgeable,” said Bocard. “The services are an excellent value, and our advice to other schools is to contact them.”</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-faf4e34 e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"faf4e34\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-8d9a500 elementor-widget elementor-widget-button\" data-id=\"8d9a500\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"button.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<a class=\"elementor-button elementor-button-link elementor-size-sm\" href=\"https://www.automworx.com/contact/\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-content-wrapper\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-text\">Contact Us</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</a>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-e624f68 elementor-widget elementor-widget-button\" data-id=\"e624f68\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"button.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<a class=\"elementor-button elementor-button-link elementor-size-sm\" href=\"https://www.automworx.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/e11d3b_8d7cb5d8348746619d2100bd40ace4a8.pdf\" target=\"_blank\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-content-wrapper\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-text\">Download the Case Study</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</a>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t"

class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">HNI Corporation converts 3,000 workflows, 2700 scheduled jobs, 400 variables, and 20 agent groups with AutomWorx</h2>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-c9e6a33 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"c9e6a33\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>HNI Corporation is a large manufacturer specializing in office furniture and hearth products founded in 1944 as an employee-owned industrial enterprise. Today, though the company has expanded their presence globally and become a Fortune 500 company, they still are headquartered in Muscatine, Iowa and most personnel are also shareholders.</p><p>An early adopter of automation tools, HNI had been successfully using the AppWorx automation platform for decades. The company had also invested heavily in enterprise-wide technology and created tight integrations across applications. Yet, in February of 2020, they were faced with the fact that by the end of the year, their version of AppWorx would no longer be functional which would jeopardize critical business functions. Their 2 best options: either upgrade to a newer version of AppWorx, or migrate to a new tool.</p><p>Either way, this meant that in just 10 months, the team would be tasked with converting over 3,000 workflows, 2700 scheduled jobs, 400 variables, 20 agent groups, along with schedule objects, connection objects, and login objects. They also had to do this across three environments: development, quality assurance, and production.</p><p>With no time to lose, and well aware of the many challenges that they were facing, HNI chose AutomWorx to keep their business running.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-e91607a e-con-full e-flex Gunlocke, Heat &amp; Glo, Harman, Quadra-Fire, and more.</p><p><strong>Problem</strong><br />Large conversion of business automation in a short amount of time.</p><p><strong>Solution<br /></strong>Partnership with AutomWorx to get the project done ahead of schedule.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element HNI Corporation. “We were in a position where we needed a partner who we could really trust, especially as many of our internal resources were already committed to other projects.”</p><p>Complicating the project were not just the time constraints but the many customizations HNI had made to both their ERP system and to AppWorx over the years. AutomWorx had to address these customizations when converting HNI’s AppWorx objects to ensure that jobs would still run correctly.</p><p>Working together, HNI and AutomWorx came up with a strategy to convert HNI from AppWorx v5 to Automic v12. AutomWorx consultants were familiar with both the soon-to-be deprecated version of AppWorx and the newest version of Automic OneAutomation. AutomWorx consultants also have deep application specialization, so they were able to navigate HNI’s Oracle environment. Once that work was completed, Leacox was able to import the files into an Automic client, using a standard utility program.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-8bb6d5f elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"8bb6d5f\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">“Every time I thought we’d hit a show-stopper, AutomWorx would come up with a solution that kept the conversion moving,” said Leacox.</h4>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-ed8bea3 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"ed8bea3\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>In November of 2020, HNI installed the final wave of jobs in Automic, going live with well over a month to spare and just in time for year-end processing.</p><p>“The implementation was virtually flawless. We had high expectations of the AutomWorx team from the start, but it went better than we even expected, and AutomWorx made all of us involved successful,” he said. “Actually, though I am happy we are live, I miss working with them.”</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-dd02187 e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"dd02187\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-44de7f6 elementor-widget elementor-widget-button\" data-id=\"44de7f6\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"button.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<a class=\"elementor-button elementor-button-link elementor-size-sm\" href=\"https://www.automworx.com/contact/\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-content-wrapper\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-text\">Contact Us</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</a>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-cc2b4d4 elementor-widget elementor-widget-button\" data-id=\"cc2b4d4\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"button.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<a class=\"elementor-button elementor-button-link elementor-size-sm\" href=\"https://www.automworx.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/e11d3b_9b6216313cac4fddbc32d4f2c2067022.pdf\" target=\"_blank\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-content-wrapper\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<span class=\"elementor-button-text\">Download the Case Study</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</span>\n\t\t\t\t\t</a>\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t"

e-con e-parent\" data-id=\"ecbfcfa\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<div class=\"e-con-inner\">\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-a62f152 e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"a62f152\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-53f61fb elementor-widget__width-initial elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"53f61fb\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h2 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">AvMed Achieves True IT Modernization and Automates HealthRules with AutomWorx\n</h2>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-4140fce elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"4140fce\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>Transforming the IT and application infrastructure of an organization that’s been operating for more than a half century is no easy task, but that’s exactly the challenge AvMed took on more than two years ago.</p><p>“It was not unlike performing a heart transplant,” said Tony Puccio, Applications Architect at AvMed, referring to the project.</p><p>Founded in 1969 as Aviation Medicine, AvMed began as a healthcare option for Miami-area pilots. Now with nearly a quarter of a million members in 30 counties, it is one of Florida’s oldest and largest non-for-profit health plans, as well as one the most highly rated. The company offers coverage for individuals and families, through employer-sponsored plans, and via Medicare.</p><p>To ensure their workload automation solution was upgraded and integrated with new critical applications, AvMed chose AutomWorx.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-43389af e-con-full e-flex e-con e-parent\" data-id=\"43389af\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-94d3969 e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"94d3969\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-0b36c2a e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"0b36c2a\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\" data-settings=\"{&quot;background_background&quot;:&quot;classic&quot;}\">\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-37c513d e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"37c513d\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\" data-settings=\"{&quot;background_background&quot;:&quot;classic&quot;}\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-1b7d6f0 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"1b7d6f0\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">About AvMed</h4>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-111b146 elementor-widget elementor-widget-image\" data-id=\"111b146\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"image.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<img loading=\"lazy\" decoding=\"async\" width=\"700\" height=\"216\" src=\"https://www.automworx.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/avmed_logo_stacked_2c.avif\" class=\"attachment-full size-full wp-image-996\" alt=\"\" srcset=\"https://www.automworx.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/avmed_logo_stacked_2c.avif 700w, https://www.automworx.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/05/avmed_logo_stacked_2c-300x93.avif 300w\" sizes=\"(max-width: 700px) 100vw, 700px\" />\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-b2e1183 elementor-widget__width-initial elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"b2e1183\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>AvMed is a Florida-based health plan headquartered in Miami with regional offices in Fort Lauderdale, Gainesville and Orlando. Approximately 230,000 Members count on AvMed for their Employer Group, Medicare and Individual and Family plan coverage.</p><p><strong>Problem<br /></strong>Aging application created operational and automation challenges.</p><p><strong>Solution<br /></strong>Application migration and workload automation converstion with AutomWorx.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-cb9f8a7 e-flex e-con-boxed e-con e-parent\" data-id=\"cb9f8a7\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<div class=\"e-con-inner\">\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-235a1bf e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"235a1bf\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-f5f5c30 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"f5f5c30\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h2 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">Changing Application Landscape</h2>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-5225943 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"5225943\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>As a health plan administrator, AvMed had relied heavily on AMISYS Advance as the company’s central application. AMISYS Advance was designed for healthcare payers and had been used widely in the industry, but over time it became less effective in meeting AvMed’s requirements. With AMISYS intellectual property starting to fade, AvMed decided to look for a new Payer Engine and ultimately selected HealthEdge HealthRules. They were also faced with an aging database, and needed get from Oracle 11 to Oracle Database 12c and SQL Server Database in order to support the new application stack.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-8d21abc elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"8d21abc\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h4 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">\n\"Even after we went live, we found AutomWorx very helpful when we ran into questions. If I could give them a rating higher than 10 out of 10, I would.”<br>\n—Tony Puccio, Applications Architect, AvMed</h4>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-7e80e52 elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"7e80e52\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>Inside of AMISYS, Puccio and his team had automated over 400 workloads with Broadcom’s AppWorx tool, and many of these workloads would need to be rebuilt in HealthRules. Puccio, in the spirit of the larger modernization project, chose to migrate from AppWorx to Automic Workload Automation, a more robust scheduling application that is also part of the Broadcom portfolio.</p><p>“We contacted AutomWorx because I knew how much experience they have with business applications, and there is no consulting firm out there who has more experience with AppWorx, UC4, or Automic,” Puccio said. “We weren’t disappointed. The conversion went nearly flawlessly, and projects actually finished ahead of time.”</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-ee0a6e3 elementor-widget elementor-widget-heading\" data-id=\"ee0a6e3\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h3 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">Consulting Approach</h3>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-fc1101b elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"fc1101b\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>AutomWorx was founded by veterans of the workload automation industry and takes a different approach to consulting than many traditional systems integrators. With deep technical experience with AppWorx, UC4, and Automic solutions, AutomWorx can advise on the workload automation platform that will best help businesses meet their goals. Central to every engagement is hands-on training to ensure that customers can use upgraded or new workload automation software effectively.</p><p>“My team watched the entire conversion of our AppWorx jobs to AWA, and the consultant explained each step,” said Puccio. “This was the beginning of what was an extremely efficient knowledge transfer.”</p><p>Though AutomWorx empowers customer to manage their own environments, they also provide extended support plans and options for ongoing maintenance and can deliver on consulting services throughout the entire product lifecycle.</p><p>“Even after we went live, we found AutomWorx very helpful when we ran into questions,” Puccio said. “If I could give them a rating higher than 10 out of 10, I would.”</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-590379f e-con-full e-flex e-con e-child\" data-id=\"590379f\" data-element_type=\"container\" data-e-type=\"container\">\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-bc8ba57 elementor-widget elementor-widget-button\" data-id=\"bc8ba57\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"button.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<a class=\"elementor-button elementor-button-link elementor-size-sm\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"heading.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t<h2 class=\"elementor-heading-title elementor-size-default\">How We Work On AppWorx, UC4, and Automic Upgrades</h2>\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element elementor-element-33d9e2e elementor-widget elementor-widget-text-editor\" data-id=\"33d9e2e\" data-element_type=\"widget\" data-e-type=\"widget\" data-widget_type=\"text-editor.default\">\n\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t<p>​​In addition to Automic software upgrades, our consultants are also highly proficient at helping you upgrade your underlying operating systems, database platforms, and other third-party software utilities required to run your Automic environment.</p><p>We ensure a thorough and accurate knowledge transfer to empower you and your team to manage your environment, but we&#8217;re still here for <span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\"><a href=\"https://www.automworx.com/contact/\">support</a></span>.</p><p>​Our consultants provide pre- or post-upgrade workshops to administrators and end users to help you understand what’s new between the current Automic software version you run and the target version you will upgrade to.</p><p>If you do not currently have Automic Workload installed, we will help you understand why it might be the right time for your business to move to this version:</p><ul><li>Automic Workload application is <span style=\"text-decoration: underline;\"><a href=\"https://expert.broadcom.com/partner/automworx-inc\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener\">Broadcom’s</a></span> go-to-market recommendation.</li><li>Automic Workload is heavily developed so that customers can take advantage of the latest technologies.</li><li>Automic Workload is a true enterprise software product that introduces many features that do not exist in other workload platforms, including AppWorx.</li></ul><p>Just some of the features that Automic Workload offers include:</p><ul><li>Active/active Automation Engine (master) Configuration as an HA Solution</li><li>Support for multiple databases (Oracle, MS SQL, PostgreSQL and DB2)</li><li>Support for the majority of OS hardware offered in the market</li><li>Multi-client technology to help minimize the requirements of multiple non- production masters/environments</li><li>Analytics Reporting and Dashboards</li><li>OS Agent can run with lower Automic software versions so there is no need to upgrade all your components at once</li><li>Zero Downtime Upgrade (ZDU)</li><li>Centralized Agent Upgrade (CAU) to help you schedule OS agent upgrades</li><li>Updated Rapid Automation Agents, including:<ul><li>WebServices for both REST and SOAP calls</li><li>Oracle E-Business Suite, Oracle PeopleSoft, Oracle Hyperion, Oracle JD Edwards</li><li>Ellucian Banner</li><li>FTP, Informatica, and others</li></ul></li><li>API-enabled</li></ul><p>Depending on the version of you may have in place, AutomWorx consultants may recommend that you migrate your existing job configurations to the Automic Workload Automation platform. Our senior consultants have written and successfully deployed the migration utilities needed to mitigate business disruption.</p>\t\t\t\t\t\t\t\t</div>\n\t\t\t\t<div class=\"elementor-element This Statement of Privacy applies to www.automworx.com, and AutomWorx, Inc and governs data collection and usage. For the purposes of this Privacy Policy, unless otherwise noted, all references to AutomWorx, Inc or AutomWorx include www.automworx.com and AutomWorx. The AutomWorx website is a consulting services informational site. By using the AutomWorx website, you consent to the data practices described in this statement.</p>\n<p>Collection of your Personal Information In order to better provide you with products and services offered, AutomWorx may collect personally identifiable information, such as your:</p>\n<p>&#8211; First and Last Name <br />&#8211; E-mail Address <br />&#8211; Phone Number <br />&#8211; Employer</p>\n<p>We do not collect any personal information about you unless you voluntarily provide it to us. To wit, we will use your information for, but not limited to, communicating with you in relation to services and/or products you have requested from us. We also may gather additional personal or non-personal information in the future.</p>\n<p><strong>Use of your Personal Information</strong></p>\n<p>AutomWorx collects and uses your personal information to operate and deliver the services you have requested.</p>\n<p>AutomWorx may also use your personally identifiable information to inform you of other products or services available from AutomWorx and its affiliates.</p>\n<p>Sharing Information with Third Parties AutomWorx does not sell, rent or lease its customer lists to third parties.</p>\n<p>AutomWorx may share data with trusted partners to help perform statistical analysis, send you email or postal mail, provide customer support, or arrange for deliveries. All such third parties are prohibited from using your personal information except to provide these services to AutomWorx, and they are required to maintain the confidentiality of your information.</p>\n<p>AutomWorx may disclose your personal information, without notice, if required to do so by law or in the good faith belief that such action is necessary to: (a) conform to the edicts of the law or comply with legal process served on AutomWorx or the site; (b) protect and defend the rights or property of AutomWorx; and/or (c) act under exigent circumstances to protect the personal safety of users of AutomWorx, or the public.</p>\n<p><strong>Tracking User Behavior</strong></p>\n<p>AutomWorx may keep track of the websites and pages our users visit within AutomWorx, in order to determine what AutomWorx services are the most popular. This data is used to deliver customized content and advertising within AutomWorx to customers whose behavior indicates that they are interested in a particular subject area. </p>\n<p><strong>Automatically Collected Information</strong></p>\n<p>Information about your computer hardware and software may be automatically collected by AutomWorx. This information can include: your IP address, browser type, domain names, access times and referring website addresses. This information is used for the operation of the service, to maintain quality of the service, and to provide general statistics regarding use of the AutomWorx website. </p>\n<p><strong>Security of your Personal Information</strong></p>\n<p>AutomWorx secures your personal information from unauthorized access, use, or disclosure. AutomWorx uses the following methods for this purpose:</p>\n<p>&#8211; SSL Protocol</p>\n<p>When personal information (such as a credit card number) is transmitted to other websites, it is protected through the use of encryption, such as the Secure Sockets Layer (SSL) protocol.</p>\n<p>We strive to take appropriate security measures to protect against unauthorized access to or alteration of your personal information. Unfortunately, no data transmission over the Internet or any wireless network can be guaranteed to be 100% secure. As a result, while we strive to protect your personal information, you acknowledge that: (a) there are security and privacy limitations inherent to the Internet which are beyond our control; and (b) security, integrity, and privacy of any and all information and data exchanged between you and us through this Site cannot be guaranteed.</p>\n<p><strong>Right to Deletion</strong></p>\n<p>Subject to certain exceptions set out below, on receipt of a verifiable request from you, we will:</p>\n<p>• Delete your personal information from our records; and <br />• Direct any service providers to delete your personal information from their records.</p>\n<p>Please note that we may not be able to comply with requests to delete your personal information if it is necessary to:</p>\n<ul>\n<li>Complete the transaction for which the personal information was collected, fulfill the terms of a written warranty or product recall conducted in accordance with federal law, provide a good or service requested by you, or reasonably anticipated within the context of our ongoing business relationship with you, or otherwise perform a contract between you and us; </li>\n<li>Detect security incidents, protect against malicious, deceptive, fraudulent, or illegal activity;
